24/06/21, 10:49 - Support Technician 25: As agreed with @Company Employee 16 , we need to change the policy in the Whatsapp chat a little, new rules shall apply:
Issues reported by Atlas shall not by fixed by SSI if Atlas can self resolve it. If Atlas is not capable to do SSI can highlight (e.g. check dialogue MF12345) 
SSI shall not quick fix something because it’s faster and easier for the daily operation. It’s OK to invest 20 minutes for checking and Atlas is subsequently independent.
Issues reported by Atlas that are a real OIL shall be treated as an OIL (Excel, ITS, JIRA).
Main target is to get Atlas into the possibility to be independent, the chat will get inactivated sooner or later. 
Thank you for your cooperation at both sides.
